Lemma B.25
.

Assume
Q\leq M,L<\ell\leq d_{2}+1
and
C_{2}<\min(d_{1},\ell)
.

Let
P=P_{1}\otimes P_{2}\otimes P_{3}\otimes\cdots
be the state

P=\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{d_{1}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes C_{2}}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{1}\\
y_{1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{1}\\
y_{L}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
y_{L+1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
y_{d_{2}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes\infty},

where

\displaystyle x_{j}=\begin{cases}d_{2}+d_{1}-M+1-j&\text{if}~j\leq d_{1}-M,\\
d_{1}+1-j&\text{if}~j>d_{1}-M,\end{cases}

\displaystyle y_{j}=d_{2}+1-j.

Let
\widetilde{C}=\min(d_{1},\ell)
.
Then,

(i)

if
C_{2}+L>\widetilde{C}-M-1
then
T_{\ell}(P)=

u_{1}^{\otimes\widetilde{C}}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{d_{1}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes C_{2}+\min(d_{2},\ell)-\widetilde{C}}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{1}\\
y_{1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{1}\\
y_{L}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
y_{L+1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
y_{d_{2}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes\infty};

(ii)

if
C_{2}+L=\widetilde{C}-M-1
then
T_{\ell}(P)=

\displaystyle u_{1}^{\otimes\widetilde{C}}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{d_{1}-M-1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{d_{1}-M+1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{d_{1}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}

\displaystyle\quad\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes C_{2}+\ell-\widetilde{C}}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{1}\\
x_{d_{1}-M}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{1}\\
y_{1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{1}\\
y_{L-1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
y_{L}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
y_{d_{2}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes\infty}.

Proof.

We use the same notation as in the proof of Lemma
B.24
.
Similar to the proof of Lemma
B.24
,
we have that

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\widetilde{P}_{d_{1}+C_{2}}

\displaystyle=u_{1}^{\otimes\widetilde{C}}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\cdots\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
x_{d_{1}+C_{2}-\widetilde{C}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}

\displaystyle u(d_{1}+C_{2})

\displaystyle=\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}^{\ell}\\
\overline{1}^{\ell-\widetilde{C}+C_{2}}(x_{d_{1}-j})_{j=0}^{\widetilde{C}-C_{2}-1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\,.

•

Assume

so
all
of the members from
(x_{d_{1}-j})_{j=0}^{M-1}
will be put down.
In particular,
u(d_{1}+\widetilde{C})
will contain no members from

.

Let

\mathfrak{p}=u_{1}^{\otimes\mathfrak{c}_{1}}\otimes\bigotimes_{j=1}^{\mathfrak{d}_{1}-M}\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\

\mathfrak{x}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes\mathfrak{c}_{2}}\otimes\bigotimes_{j=\mathfrak{d}_{1}-M+1}^{\mathfrak{d}_{1}+\mathfrak{d}_{2}-M}\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathfrak{w}_{j}\\
\mathfrak{y}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes\infty}

with
\mathfrak{c}_{2}\geq\mathfrak{d}_{1}>\mathfrak{d}_{2}
.

.

\mathfrak{x}_{j}\\

\mathfrak{y}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes\infty}.

\mathfrak{y}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\bigotimes_{j=\mathfrak{d}_{1}+\mathfrak{d}_{2}-M-t+t_{1}+1}^{\mathfrak{d}_{1}+\mathfrak{d}_{2}-M}\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
\mathfrak{y}_{j}\\

t>t_{0}
, we apply Lemma
B.25

(i)
to find that

\hline\cr\end{array}

\mathfrak{y}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes\infty}.

to find that
(T_{\ell})^{t}(\mathfrak{p})=

\displaystyle u_{1}^{\otimes\mathfrak{c}_{1}+(\mathfrak{d}_{2}+1)t_{0}+\mathfrak{d}_{2}(t-t_{0})}\otimes\bigotimes_{j=1}^{\mathfrak{d}_{2}-M}\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
\mathfrak{y}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\bigotimes_{j=1}^{M}\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
\mathfrak{x}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes t-M-L+\mathfrak{d}_{2}-t_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\otimes\bigotimes_{j=\mathfrak{d}_{2}-M+1}^{2\mathfrak{d}_{2}-M}\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathfrak{w}_{j+\mathfrak{d}_{1}-\mathfrak{d}_{2}}\\
\mathfrak{y}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\bigotimes_{j=2\mathfrak{d}_{2}-M+1}^{\mathfrak{d}_{1}+\mathfrak{d}_{2}-M}\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\overline{2}\\
\mathfrak{y}_{j}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes u_{1}^{\otimes\infty}.

Observe that the phase shift is
2\mathfrak{d}_{2}-L-M
, as required.

\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}\,.

(Note that
\widetilde{a}_{3}\geq\widetilde{b}_{2}>0

This insertion is the same as in (

).
Therefore, for these cases,

R\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell}\\

\mathbf{2}^{\widetilde{b}_{2}-1}\mathbf{1}^{\widetilde{b}_{1}}\mathbf{y}\mathbf{0}^{b_{0}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\\
\,.

Making the substitutions for (

\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}.

\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{x}_{0}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}.

(

1

b)
.

a_{1}>0
then

\displaystyle\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell}\\

\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{2}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{2}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}+a_{1}}&\\
\mathbf{z}_{\ell-1}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}

\displaystyle=\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell-a_{1}-1}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=2}^{a_{3}+a_{2}}\overline{1}\\
\overline{1}^{b_{2}}\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{1}}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-a_{1}-1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{a_{1}+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{2}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+2}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}+1}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=\ell-a_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{z}_{\ell-a_{1}}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}.

Now, regardless of whether
a_{1}>0
or
a_{1}=0
,

\displaystyle\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell}\\
\overline{2}^{a_{3}-1}\overline{1}^{a_{2}+1}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\\
\overline{1}^{b_{2}}\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{1}}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}\\
\overline{2}\\
\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=2}^{b_{2}+b_{1}+1}\\
\overline{1}^{b_{2}}\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{1}-1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{\ell-b_{2}-b_{1}+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{2}(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+2}^{a_{3}+a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{1}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}&\text{if}~b_{1}>0\\
\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=2}^{b_{2}+b_{1}+1}\\
\overline{1}^{b_{2}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{\ell-b_{2}-b_{1}+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{2}(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+2}^{a_{3}+a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{1}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}&\text{if}~b_{1}=0\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{b_{2}}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=2}^{b_{2}+1}\\
\overline{1}^{b_{2}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{\ell-b_{2}+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{2}(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+2}^{a_{3}+a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{1}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{1}}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}

\displaystyle=\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{\ell+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{2}(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=2}^{a_{3}+a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{1}^{b_{2}+1}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}=\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{\ell+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{2}^{a_{3}}\overline{1}^{a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{1}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}.

This above insertion is the same as (
D.1
), proving
(

¯
1

b)
.
∎

Proof of
(

¯
1

a)
and
(

n

b)
.

Let
\mathbf{v}=\overline{1}
for
(

¯
1

a)
and
\mathbf{v}=\mathbf{x}_{0}
for
(

n

b)
.
Under the assumptions for (
D.1
) or (
D.2
),
additionally assume that
b_{1}>0
and
\mathbf{y}\geq\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}}
if
\mathbf{v}=\overline{1}
.

Set,

(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}=\begin{cases}\overline{1}^{b_{2}+1}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{1}-1}&\text{for~\ref{enum:bona}}\\
\mathbf{x}_{0}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{1}-1}&\text{for~\ref{enum:fb}}\end{cases}

Now,

\displaystyle\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=1}^{a_{3}+a_{2}-1}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\\
(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}\\
\mathbf{h}_{a_{3}+a_{2}}\\
\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}

\displaystyle=\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell-1}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=1}^{a_{3}+a_{2}-1}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}}^{b_{1}+a_{1}-1}\\
(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-2}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{2}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{h}_{a_{3}+a_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}+a_{1}}&\\
\mathbf{z}_{\ell-1}&\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell-a_{1}-1}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=1}^{a_{3}+a_{2}-1}\\
(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{a_{1}+2}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{h}_{a_{3}+a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}&\text{if $b_{2}+b_{1}=a_{3}+a_{2}-1$}\\
\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell-a_{1}-1}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=1}^{a_{3}+a_{2}-1}\\
(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-a_{1}-2}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{a_{1}+2}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{h}_{a_{3}+a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=\ell-a_{1}}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{z}_{\ell-a_{1}-1}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}&\text{if $b_{2}+b_{1}\neq a_{3}+a_{2}-1$}\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\col\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\\
(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)\rightarrow\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{\ell-b_{2}-b_{1}+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil(\mathbf{h}_{a_{3}+a_{2}})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{a_{3}+a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}

\displaystyle=\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{\ell+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=1}^{a_{3}+a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\mathbf{x}_{b_{1}}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}=\begin{array}[]{|c|c|c}\hline\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{X}^{\ell+1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\overline{2}^{a_{3}}\overline{1}^{a_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol\\
\cline{3-3}\cr\vrule\lx@intercol\hfil\mathbf{v}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=1}^{\ell-1}\hfil\lx@intercol\vrule\lx@intercol&\\
\cline{2-2}\cr\mathbf{y}\\
\cline{1-1}\cr\end{array}

This insertion is the same as in (
D.1
) or (
D.2
).
Therefore,

R\left(\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=1}^{\ell}\\
(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=1}^{\ell}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}\\
\mathbf{v}\\
\mathbf{y}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\right)=\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}\\
\mathbf{h}_{a_{3}+a_{2}}\\
\mathbf{z}_{\ell}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}\\
\otimes\begin{array}[]{|c|}\hline\cr\mathbf{X}^{\ell}\\
(\mathbf{h}_{j})_{j=1}^{a_{3}+a_{2}-1}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=b_{1}+1}^{b_{1}+a_{1}}\\
(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{z}_{j})_{j=b_{2}+b_{1}+1}^{\ell-1}\\
\hline\cr\end{array}

Substituting
\mathbf{v}=\overline{1}
and
(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}=\overline{1}^{b_{2}+1}(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{1}-1}

proves
(

¯
1

a)
.

Substituting
\mathbf{v}=\mathbf{x}_{0}
and
(\widetilde{\mathbf{z}}_{j})_{j=1}^{b_{2}+b_{1}}=(\mathbf{x}_{j})_{j=0}^{b_{1}-1}

proves
(

n

b)
.
∎
